## Standing rule — FINALE COUNTDOWN (added 2026-11-23, user-approved; applies to ALL publishing slots)
- When the episode being published is EP048 of its series: replace the slot's usual story text with 倒计时！只剩2集就大结局了，你猜结局会怎样？
- EP049: story text 只剩最后1集了！大结局你希望怎么收尾？
- EP050 (finale): story text 大结局来了！今晚一起守候，你希望是甜蜜收尾还是意难平？ AND publish ONE extra finale post (poster image + text) on FB profile, FB Page and Threads: 大结局收官！陪伴我们这么久的故事今晚落幕，你给这部剧打几分？最舍不得哪个角色？ + the day's hashtag set (Threads: keep ≤500 chars, trim hashtags from the end). Record the finale post IDs here. This rule overrides the slot's normal story text for those three episodes only.

## Standing rule — FRAME STORY ALTERNATION (added 2026-11-23, user-approved; applies to ALL publishing slots)
- On EVEN-numbered days of the month, build the slot's IG/FB story image from a real frame of today's episode instead of the poster copy: ffmpeg -ss <35% of the episode's duration> -i <episode file> -frames:v 1, scale/crop to 1080x1920 (center crop), then burn the slot's usual story text with the standard drawtext recipe (NotoSerifCJK-Bold, white, black@0.6 box, position avoiding faces/text). On ODD-numbered days, use the poster copy as usual. If frame extraction or cropping fails, fall back to the poster copy and note it.

## Standing rule — SEED COMMENT ROTATION (added 2026-11-23, user-approved; applies to ALL slots; OVERRIDES the fixed seed-comment text in the slot bodies)
The FB profile seed comment on each episode post uses the question at index (episode number mod 6), counting from 0: (0) 第{EP}集你最喜欢哪个片段？欢迎在评论区一起讨论剧情！ (1) 第{EP}集哪一幕最让你心疼？说说你的感受！ (2) 第{EP}集的反转你猜到了吗？有没有被剧情吓一跳？ (3) 第{EP}集里你最喜欢哪句台词？打在评论区吧！ (4) 如果你是第{EP}集的主角，你会做同样的选择吗？ (5) 第{EP}集你给几分？满分10分你打多少？ Then append: 关注 HeroDrama，每天更新精彩短剧。 plus ONE series-title hashtag: "#" + the series title from today's poster filename (strip the leading number/underscore prefix and the file extension; remove punctuation ，。！？、· and spaces).

## Standing rule — SERIES TRANSITION (added 2026-11-23, user-approved; applies to ALL slots)
- AUDIENCE VOTE: when a slot publishes EP048 of a series (and another series remains in the cycle), that slot ALSO publishes a vote post on Threads, IG feed and FB Page: a combined image of the next three unpublished posters (ffmpeg hstack at equal heights) with caption: 下一部追什么？你说了算！在评论区写下你最想看的海报编号，得票最高的我们重点推荐！ + the day's hashtag set (Threads ≤500 chars, trim hashtags). Record the vote post IDs and the three poster numbers here.
- LAUNCH PACKAGE: when a slot publishes EP001 of Series_02, Series_03 or Series_04 (the first episode of a new series), that slot ALSO: (1) publishes a launch post on IG feed + FB Page with the episode's poster: 新剧开播！第一集来了，这部你期待吗？关注 HeroDrama，一起从头追！ + the day's hashtag set; (2) uses story text 新剧开播！第一集你看了吗？ instead of the slot's usual story text; (3) if an audience vote was held for this transition, adds to the launch post: 还记得之前的投票吗？得票最高的海报就是它！ Record everything here.
